- **testing-specialist.guards.knowledge**: - A **method whose RETURN TYPE widens** (`Future&lt;void&gt;`→`Future&lt;bool&gt;`) — every existing call site discards it and the suite stays green under `return true`. - **theseus**: Theseus — the debugger of the Olympus family. Give it a bug, a failing test, or unexpected behavior and it follows the thread through the codebase to the root cause, then fixes it at the source. Reads, writes, and runs. Use it when something is broken and you need the *why*, not just a patch over the symptom. - **thesis-architect**: Sharpens the controlling idea before the outline is drawn. - **thoughtbox-analyst**: Persistent background teammate that monitors Thoughtbox sessions for evolution candidates, contradiction detection, and session digests. - **thoughts-analyzer**: The research equivalent of codebase-analyzer. Use this subagent_type when wanting to deep dive on a research topic. Not commonly needed otherwise. - **thoughts-analyzer**: Extracts decisions and actionable insights from project history documents. Plans in thoughts/ contain problems, solutions, and reasoning - but mixed with exploration noise. - **thoughts-analyzer**: Extracts decisions, constraints, and specs from specific `thoughts/` documents you already have paths to, and flags whether they still hold. Use to distil what a draft design, plan, or research doc proposed — speculative material whose staleness must be inferred. Not for executed decisions of record (use docs-analyzer for ADRs/feature docs), finding which documents exist (use thoughts-locator), or analyzing code (use codebase-analyzer). ([samjmarshall/rekurve](https://github.com/samjmarshall/rekurve/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/thoughts-analyzer.md) · ★3)

- **thoughts-analyzer**: Deeply reads one or more `thoughts/` documents and extracts the key insights, decisions, and outstanding questions. - **thoughts-analyzer**: The research equivalent of codebase-analyzer. Use when wanting to deep dive on a research topic. ([paxsonsa/myskillz](https://github.com/paxsonsa/myskillz/blob/HEAD/paxdev/agents/thoughts-analyzer.md))
- **thoughts-explorer**: Discovers and analyzes documents in ~/.claude/thoughts/ directory. Use when researching historical context, decisions, or documentation for any topic. - **thoughts-locator**: Finds and categorizes documents in the `thoughts/` directory — designs, plans, research notes, PR write-ups, todos. Use when a task needs speculative or historical context: prior explorations, draft designs, or discussions on a topic. Returns grouped paths with dates, not contents. - **thread-ml-interpreter**: 模型可解釋性的唯讀分析 Thread。產出 Global 與 Local 解釋、跨 folds 的穩定性、失敗模式與 counterfactual，並嚴格區分「模型依賴什麼」與「什麼造成什麼」。當要說明模型靠什麼做決定、要回答某一筆預測為什麼是這個結果、要準備人工複核或客訴用的 reason code、要做公平性與受保護屬性代理檢查，或要在上線前補齊 Model Card 的風險段時派它。 ([cdexswzaq0110/Serendipity-Epiphany](https://github.com/cdexswzaq0110/Serendipity-Epiphany/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/thread-ml-interpreter.md))
- **thread-reviewer-spec**: Spec 軸審查 Thread（Reviewer A）。只檢查一件事——做出來的東西是不是規格要的那個東西。與 thread-reviewer-standards 同時派出、首輪互不參照。當一組變更完成、要對照規格或 Process 驗收時使用。 ([cdexswzaq0110/Serendipity-Epiphany](https://github.com/cdexswzaq0110/Serendipity-Epiphany/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/thread-reviewer-spec.md))
- **thread-reviewer-standards**: Standards 軸審查 Thread（Reviewer B）。只檢查一件事——東西做得好不好：正確性、測試可信度、資料流、命名、耦合、安全、效能、code smell 基線。與 thread-reviewer-spec 同時派出、首輪互不參照。當一組變更完成、要做品質審查時使用。 ([cdexswzaq0110/Serendipity-Epiphany](https://github.com/cdexswzaq0110/Serendipity-Epiphany/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/thread-reviewer-standards.md))
- **thread-sa**: 業務規則梳理的唯讀分析 Thread（SA 角色）。把「系統怎麼判斷」寫成可判定的規則表，含判定條件、例外、邊界與衝突。當要定義系統的判斷邏輯、要盤點既有規則散落在哪、要處理模糊地帶怎麼辦，或發現 Dev 在自己發明業務規則時派它。 ([cdexswzaq0110/Serendipity-Epiphany](https://github.com/cdexswzaq0110/Serendipity-Epiphany/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/thread-sa.md))
- **thread-safety-auditor**: Audits Class F — non-Sync interior mutability (`Cell`/`RefCell`/`UnsafeCell`/`Rc`) on a `#[pyclass]` payload that the always-on `threading` blanket forces `Send+Sync`, compiling only via a hand-written `unsafe impl Sync`. Under free-threaded Python (`PYTHON_GIL=0`) two threads race it: `RefCell` → `BorrowMutError` panic, `Cell`/`UnsafeCell` → torn read / UB.\n\n&lt;example&gt;\nUser: Is RustPython's contextvars thread-safe under free-threading?\nAgent: I will run scan_thread_safety.py, confirm the RefCell/Cell/UnsafeCell payloads carry a hand-written `unsafe impl Sync`, and rank them — RefCell payl… ([ReviewToolkits/rustpy-review-toolkit](https://github.com/ReviewToolkits/rustpy-review-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/rustpy-review-toolkit/agents/thread-safety-auditor.md) · ★2)
- **thread-scout**: 唯讀廣度搜尋 Thread。當回答一個問題需要掃過很多檔案、目錄或命名慣例，而你只要結論不要檔案內容時派它。它定位程式碼，不審查也不稽核。 ([cdexswzaq0110/Serendipity-Epiphany](https://github.com/cdexswzaq0110/Serendipity-Epiphany/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/thread-scout.md))
- **thread-security**: 安全敏感變更的唯讀稽核 Thread。對照信任邊界、認證授權、秘密處理、輸入驗證、資料邊界、供應鏈與部署設定做威脅分析。當變更碰到 auth、使用者輸入、憑證、對外介面、檔案路徑、序列化、或部署設定時派它。 ([cdexswzaq0110/Serendipity-Epiphany](https://github.com/cdexswzaq0110/Serendipity-Epiphany/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/thread-security.md))
- **thread-supervisor**: 承諾邊界上的唯讀顧問 Thread，用你能拿到最強的推理配置。只做策略、分解批判、風險與品味判斷——從不執行。當兩個 Worker 結果矛盾、同一個子任務驗證失敗兩次、判斷落在成功條件之外、或計畫需要結構性改變時派它；另有兩次強制諮詢：計畫定案前與交付物成形後。 ([cdexswzaq0110/Serendipity-Epiphany](https://github.com/cdexswzaq0110/Serendipity-Epiphany/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/thread-supervisor.md))
- **thread-system-architect**: 系統設計專用的唯讀 Thread。拿到需求或既有架構，走九階段流程產出設計文件或瓶頸評估——先估數字再選架構、先找瓶頸再加元件、每個元件都要說出它引入什麼新故障模式。當要設計一個新系統、拿到 PRD 要寫架構文件、被問系統怎麼擴展、怎麼避免超賣或重複、怎麼撐住尖峰，或要評估既有架構的瓶頸時派它。 ([cdexswzaq0110/Serendipity-Epiphany](https://github.com/cdexswzaq0110/Serendipity-Epiphany/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/thread-system-architect.md))
- **thread-ux**: 使用者流程與介面規格的唯讀分析 Thread（UX / UI 角色）。產出主要流程、關鍵路徑、失敗與空狀態、介面狀態與一致性規則。當要設計或盤點使用者怎麼走完一件事、要定義畫面有哪些狀態、要檢查既有流程的斷點，或下游在自己猜使用者行為時派它。 ([cdexswzaq0110/Serendipity-Epiphany](https://github.com/cdexswzaq0110/Serendipity-Epiphany/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/thread-ux.md))
- **thread-verdict**: Judges one file's UNRESOLVED review threads against the file as it is now — fixed, standing, gone, or detached — and nothing else about the file. Posts nothing. Consolidates AND scores every facet against the canonical rubrics and writes Integral-Analysis-day0-to-day&lt;N&gt;.md. Use ONLY for the Integral Analysis session of the thread-weaver skill; never for the Digests session (which fans out per transcript), the Registers session (register appends), or the IF/Curve sessions. What it is day to day is four files in one place, a message being only a nudge. ([canmi21/canmi](https://github.com/canmi21/canmi/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/threads.md))

- **threads-director**: Директор редакции Threads: сверяет план с фактом, проверяет что вышло, ловит конфликты и устаревшие детали, шлёт один утренний бриф автору.
